Let's design internal exact objective with FFT and active weights:
```
def normalized_lse(a, tau):
    c = conv(a)
    cmax = np.max(c)
    # To avoid overflow if tau tiny:
    x = (c - cmax) / tau
    # clamp x >= -745? Or use logsumexp.
    y = np.exp(x)  # underflow ok
    s = y.sum()
    if s == 0:
       # weights on max exactly
       m = (c == cmax)
       w = np.zeros_like(c); w[m] = 1/m.sum()
    else:
       w = y/s
    val = cmax + tau*np.log(s)
    return val, w
```
For very small tau, cmax + tau log(s) may equal cmax if s≈active_count. If active_count>1 and tau small, LSE ≈ cmax + tau log(count). Gradient weights uniform on active (if exact equal). Good.

But LSE objective may be very flat direction? If active many, smoothing adds small constant. Fine.

def lse_directed_step(a, tau, step, use_exact=False):
    val, w = lse_weights(a, tau)
    g = grad(w,a)
    gm = g - g.mean()
    v = a - step*gm
    v = project_simplex(v)
    return v
```

We can combine objective and gradient: in `lse_weights(a, tau)` compute c via Fa*Fa. In gradient need Fa and Fw. We can compute both in one function to reuse Fa. Define:
```
def lse_obj_grad(a, tau):
   n=len(a); N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
   cf = fa*fa
   c = np.fft.irfft(cf, N)[:2*n-1]
   c = np.maximum(c,0)
   cmax = c.max()
   x = (c-cmax)/tau
   if x.min() < -700: x = np.maximum(x,-700)? But underflow okay.
   e = np.exp(x)
   s = e.sum()
   if s==0: ...
   val = cmax + tau*np.log(s)
   w = e/s
   fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
   g = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw, N)[:n].real
   g = 2*g
   return val, g, c, cmax
```
This computes 2 rffts + 2 irffts? fa, cf (square same length), irfft c, fw, irfft g. Total 4 transforms. Good.

But for many random perturbations, need only c/max score, no gradient: `fast_cmax(a)` two transforms (rfft, irfft). Use optimization.

Project_simplex(v): efficient O(n log n) sort. For v length 5000, sort ~0.5ms? called many -> maybe. Could use O(n) alternative? Use sort okay. Formula:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, len(v)+1) - (cssv - 1) > 0)[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1) / (rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```
This projects to sum 1. If v all negative, returns zeros sum0; then fix to uniform. Ensure sum=1.

But projected gradient with d = a - step(g-mean) may not be far; simple normalization after max: `v=np.maximum(v,0); s=v.sum(); if s>0: v/=s` can be much faster though not Euclidean projection. It preserves nonnegativity and sum, but not best and can distort. For line search objective, Euclidean projection maybe important. Could use fast approximation first, exact every few? Sort 5000 length not too bad. Let's use exact.

Need avoid modifying global arrays.

```
_LSE_TAU = 1e-5
def lbfgs_obj(x):
    a=softmax(x)
    val,_,_,_ = lse_obj_grad(a, _LSE_TAU)
    return val
def lbfgs_grad(x):
    a=softmax(x)
    val,g,_,_ = lse_obj_grad(a, _LSE_TAU)
    return a*(g - np.dot(a,g))
```
This recomputes twice per iteration. Could implement custom L-BFGS? Maybe not.

Time limit for minimize: It may ignore if maxfun high. Set maxfun based on time? We can run multiple minimize calls with maxfun 200 each and check time.

```
def lbfgs_tau(a, tau, maxfun, maxtime):
  global _LSE_TAU
  _LSE_TAU=tau
  x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
  start=time
  while time<start+maxtime:
    res=minimize(lbfgs_obj, x, jac=lbfgs_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':200,'maxfun':400,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12})
    x=res.x
    a=softmax(x)
    # check exact; if no improvement break?
  return a
```
But minimize may stop if ftol met; then no need more. Use maxiter 100.

Active λ QP approach in more detail:
Given a, select active lags A of size p (top p). We can approximate subdirection by solving for λ that minimizes variance of gradient h = A_λ a. Then step a in direction d = -(h - mean(h)). But if λ solves variance, h nearly constant, direction small. Wait if h is chosen to be as constant as possible, then d is small, no descent. Actually KKT wants h constant. If at current not at optimum, can we make h constant using active top constraints? If the minimal variance >0, then d nonzero. The direction is the average gradient under λ*; moving opposite decreases all active? It should point to reduce variation.

But if we choose λ to make h constant, the inner product with active gradients? The linearized change for constraint k is g_k·d = - (g_k·(h-mean))? We want negative. If h is average gradient, d=-(h-mean), then for active gradients g_k: g_k·d = -g_k·h + g_k·mean. Not guaranteed negative. For weighted average, h·d = -||h-mean||^2 negative. But individual active may increase. However small step reduces max in some sense.

Maybe better choose direction d as solution to minimizing max linearized increase: min max_{k∈A} g_k·d s.t. ||d||≤1, sum d=0. This is Chebyshev. Its dual is max over λ of -? Actually min_d max_k λ? The primal: min t s.t. g_k^T d ≤ t, |d| constraints. The dual: max -λ^T? If no norm, might be -inf. This direction is optimal. Hard but can approximate with λ weights where d = -∑λ g_k? For unconstrained least norm, minimizing max linear is related.

Could use trust-region: find d with limited norm that minimizes LSE gradient. That's just projected gradient if λ=softmax.

We can try several active sizes: top 1, top 5, top 20, top 50, top 200, top 1000 (uniform). Also LSE τ weights. Take best improvement. This is cheap: for each direction, compute a few line searches. Active many, direction average. This may find better than top 50 previous. For top 1000, average gradient may be smoother.

Sum over A can be computed as 2 * correlate(λ_ext, a) with λ uniform over A. This is same as LSE with uniform top p. So we can reuse weights: set w zeros, w[A]=1/p, grad=2correlate. Then d=-(g-mean). Line search exact M.

Let's detail each.

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p <<=1
    return p
```
For m=10000, p=16384.

`fast_convolve(a)`:
```
def fast_convolve(a):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
    # clip very small negative
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0? If c has negative up to -1e-16.
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
If a sum huge? We normalize sum1; fa fine. If negative values? clip input a nonnegative.

`fast_cmax(a)`:
```
if len==0 return inf
a=np.max(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 return inf? For direction line search, sum 1.
return fast_convolve(a).max()
```
For score: `2*n*M/(S*S)`. If S=1, `2*n*M`.

Potential issue: `np.fft.irfft` length N returns real; for odd/even? N power of 2. Good.

`lse_obj_grad(a, tau)`:

```
c=fast_convolve(a); cmax=c.max()
if tau <= cmax*1e-9:
   mask=c>=cmax - 1e-15*cmax? or equals; maybe all with max - 1e-12
   w=mask/sum
else:
   x=(c-cmax)/tau
   # avoid underflow: set x=np.maximum(x, -745) maybe but if many, all at -745 sum huge? Underflow to 0 is fine.

      if not mask.any(): mask[np.argmax(c)]=True
      w=mask.astype(float)/mask.sum()
   else: w=e/s
   val=cmax + tau*np.log(s)
# gradient via FFT using fa? We already computed fa in conv; but fast_convolve doesn't return fa. Modify to compute fa? For LSE, compute fa again? Could accept. Or create `lse_obj_grad` independent:
n; N; fa=rfft; c=irfft(fa*fa); ...; fw=rfft(w); g=2*irfft(conj(fa)*fw,N)[:n]

```

`lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, maxtime)`:
```
def lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, maxtime=60.0, maxfun_per=1000, maxiter=300):
    global _LSE_TAU
    _LSE_TAU=tau
    a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum()
    x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    deadline=time.monotonic()+maxtime
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=fast_cmax(a)
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       try:
          res=minimize(_lbfgs_obj, x, jac=_lbfgs_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun_per,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-14,'maxls':50})
          x=res.x
       except Exception:
          break
       a=softmax(x); m=fast_cmax(a)
       if m<best_m:
          best_m=m; best_a=a.copy(); print...
       # if not much improvement break? maybe if m > best_m + 1e-8 and iterations stop? continue maybe with new tau only.
       if time... break
    return best_a,best_m
```
Note minimizing LSE value, not exact M. It may move to lower LSE but higher exact if tau large. We track exact cmax and return best exact. But x may drift. We update x with result. If exact worsens but LSE improves, later smaller tau may recover? Maybe keep if LSE objective improves, but best exact separate. Good.

Could there be nan due tau small? Ensure.

```
def smooth_anneal(a, deadline, max_time_per=60):
  while time<deadline:
    M=fast_cmax(a)
    tau = max(1e-8, M*0.1)
    a2,m2=lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, max_time_per)
    if m2 < M: a=a2; M=m2; print
    else: # try smaller tau? or break for this phase
```

Let's implement `direct_lse_optimize(a, deadline, initial_tau=None)`:
- a normalized.
- tau = initial or M*0.1.
- lr = 0.02? Need tune.
- For each iteration until deadline or lr small:
  - val,g,c,M = lse_obj_grad(a, tau)
  - if M<best update.
  - d = -(g - g.mean())
  - scale RMS: rms = sqrt(mean(d*d)); if rms < 1e-12 break
  - d = d / max(1.0, rms*10?) Wait if rms is O0.01? We want step alpha ~1e-4. If set d RMS=1, alpha=1e-5. Let's normalize d to RMS=1: d=d/rms. Then step alpha. But gradient may have RMS maybe 0.01. If normalize, ignoring magnitude; okay with line search.
  - alpha schedule start 1e-4.
  - Line search: for trial in [alpha, alpha/2, ...8x]: v=project(a+alpha*d); m=fast_cmax(v); if m < M - 1e-15: accept; alpha=min(alpha*1.2,1e-2); break; else alpha*=0.5.
  - If accepted, tau*=0.98? (reduce smoothing) maybe after 100 accepted.
  - If 50 nonaccept, tau*=0.8; alpha*=0.8; if alpha<1e-8 break to subgradient.
```
This uses exact cmax for line search, not LSE val. It moves to reduce exact M along LSE gradient. Good.
But it may ignore LSE improvement if exact M not decrease due nonmonotonic. However for local max, likely.

Subgradient bundle direction line search is similar but with many directions. We can combine in one loop: generate a list of directions including LSE gradient and active averages; evaluate alphas; choose best. This is robust.

Let's design `bundle_polish(a, deadline)`:
For each iteration:
- Compute c,M. If M best update.
- Determine set of weights/directions:
  * LSE tau values: tau = max(1e-10, M* frac) with fracs [0.02,0.1,0.5] maybe. Get weights from lse? We don't want full gradient for all? We can compute weights via softmax; gradient direction. But lse_obj_grad per tau expensive. Maybe just top uniform.
  * top p: p in [1,2,5,10,25,50,100,250,500,1000,2000] (unique <=2n-1). For each, weights_ext zeros, set top p to 1/p. Compute g via FFT, d = normalize(-(g-mean)). 
  * random active? maybe.

def bundle_polish(a, deadline, max_candidates=...):
  a=normalize
  best_m=fast_cmax(a); best=a
  p_list=[1,3,5,10,20,50,100,200,500,1000,2000,4000]
  alphas=[0.05,0.02,0.008,0.003,0.001,0.0003]
  # dynamic p limit based on 2n
  no_improve=0
  base_alphas=...
  for it while time:
    c=fast_convolve(a); M=c.max()
    if M<best_m: best...
    # top indices only once per iteration
    order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]  # length 2n-1 ~10k, sort 10k cost maybe 0.005s. Could use np.argpartition for p max. Simpler sort okay if it ~1000 -> 5s? 10k sort 1ms.
    best_candidate=None; best_trial=M
    # Precompute weights? For each p, set w top p; could compute g via direct accumulate? For p small maybe direct? FFT easier.
    for p in unique p_list <=2n:
       w=zeros; w[order[:p]]=1/p; g=gradient_from_weights(w,a); d=-(g - g.mean()); if norm... 
       for alpha in alphas:
          v=project(a + alpha*d)
          m=fast_cmax(v)
          if m < best_trial: ...
    # Also LSE from previous? We can include p=2n (all lags uniform gradient? That's derivative of sum c = (S*?); gradient constant? Maybe skip)
    if best_trial < M:
       a=best_candidate; no_improve=0; # bicycle alphas maybe
    else:
       no_improve +=1
       # reduce alphas or try random? If no improve over all, try LSE smooth step or random hillclimb
```

Let's make fast_cmax efficient:
```
def fast_cmax(a):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
    # clip using in-place maximum? c = c.copy(); c[c<0]=0
    return float(c.max())
```
Potential memory. Fine.

`direction_from_weights(c, a, fa, w)` etc.

We need `next_pow2` called many times; can cache by n. Since n constant in a search, use global `_FFT_LEN[n]`. Use dict global.
```
_FFT_CACHE = {}
def get_fft_len(n):
    key=n
    if key not: L=2*n-1; p=1; while p<L: p<<=1; _FFT_CACHE[key]=p
    return _FFT_CACHE[key]
```

Cheap normalize:
```
def normalize_nonneg(v):
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = v.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return np.ones(n)/n
    return v / s
```

`trim_to_mass`: To find interval with given mass, compute cumulative sums. Choose smallest subarray containing mass. For nonnegative. Use sliding window two pointers O(n):
```
def trim_to_mass(a, mass=0.9999):
   a=np.maximum(a,0); total=a.sum(); if total<=0 return a
   target=mass*total
   n=len(a); i=0; best=(-1,n+1); best_len=inf
   for j in range(n):
      while target - (cum[j+1]-cum[i]) > 0: i+=1
      # now window [i,j] contains target
      if j-i+1 < best_len: best...
   return a[best_l:best_r+1]
```
Need cumulative length n+1. If target=0? okay. For mass close 1, finds minimal span. If a has zeros edges, trim. If mass=1 exactly, may return whole support.
Then normalize.
Also trim tiny values below threshold? The sliding window keeps all between min and max, even small values. Good.

```
def gaussian_smooth(a, sigma_bins):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(max(2*n-1, 4*sigma))? Use N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   x=np.arange(-n//2, n//2)
   kernel=np.exp(-0.5*(x/sigma_bins)**2); kernel/=kernel.sum()
   fa=rfft(a,N); fk=rfft(kernel,N); out=irfft(fa*fk,N)[:n]
   return max(out,0)
```

def smooth_anneal(a, deadline):
   a=normalize; best=a; best_m=fast_cmax(a)
   # start tau = M*0.2; min tau=1e-8
   while time < deadline:
      M=fast_cmax(a)
      tau = max(1e-8, 0.1*M)
      # if tau < 1e-7 maybe break to bundle
      a_cur, a_best, m_best = lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, maxtime=60)
      if m_best < best_m: best=a_best
      # decide next a: if m_best < M*1.01 use a_best else a_current? Use a_current if m_cur <= M + small? If exploded, use best.
      a = a_best if m_best < M else a_cur
      # if no exact improvement over 2 loops, break?
   return best
```

def direct_lse_steps(a, deadline, tau_frac=0.1, max_iter=5000):

def bundle_polish(a, deadline, max_seconds=None):
  a=normalize
  best=a; best_m=fast_cmax(a)
  n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=get_fft_len(n)
  p_list = [1,3,7,15,30,60,120,250,500,1000,2000,4000]
  p_list = [p for p in p_list if p<L]
  alphas = [0.05,0.02,0.008,0.003,0.001,0.0003]
  no_improve = 0
  last_best = time.monotonic()
  while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    c_a = fast_convolve(a)
    M = c_a.max()
    if M < best_m - 1e-16:
       best_m=M; best=a.copy(); last_best=time...
    # If no improvement for 20s, maybe try random perturb or break?
    if time - last_best > 30 and no_improve>200: break?
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    order = np.argsort(c_a)[::-1]
    best_trial_m = M
    best_trial = None
    # Top uniform directions
    for p in p_list:
       if p == L: w = np.ones(L)/L? (all) skip? all uniform gradient maybe constant? maybe not due triangular? g_i=2∑K a? For all weights, g_i =2∑_{r} a_r *1 =2; constant, direction zero. skip p==L.
       w = np.zeros(L); w[order[:p]] = 1.0/p
       gw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
       g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*gw, N)[:n].real
       d = -(g - g.mean())
       if np.linalg.norm(d) < 1e-15: continue
       # evaluate alphas but break if M not improved? We want best.
       for alpha in alphas:
          v = normalize_nonneg(a + alpha*d)
          m = fast_cmax(v)
          if m < best_trial_m:
             best_trial_m=m; best_trial=v
          # if m < M - some, can still continue for larger? We try smaller alphas maybe; but best among.
    # LSE tau directions
    for frac in [0.05,0.2,0.8]:
       tau = max(1e-12, M*frac)
       x=(c_a-M)/tau
       e=np.exp(np.maximum(x,-700)); s=e.sum()
       if s==0:
           p=1? already
           continue
       w=e/s
       gw=rfft(w,N); g=2*irfft(conj(fa)*gw,N)[:n]; d=-(g-g.mean())
       ...
    if best_trial is not None:
       a=best_trial; no_improve=0
       # Optional if improvement small reduce alphas? keep
    else:
       no_improve +=1
       # If stuck, try a random perturbation direction with small alpha?
       if no_improve % 10 ==0:
          d = rng.normal(0,1,n); d -= d.mean()
          # scale? Use normalize_nonneg(a+alpha*d)
          for alpha in [1e-4,1e-5,1e-6]: ...
          if improve accept
       if no_improve > 1000: break
```

Hillclimb exact pair split could find improvements bundle misses. Implement limited:
```
def random_hillclimb(a, deadline):
  a=normalize; best_m=fast_cmax; best=a
  n=len(a)
  sigma=0.01
  for it:
    # choose pair
    i,j = rng.choice...
    t=a[i]+a[j]
    if t<=0 continue
    u=a[i]/t
    # propose new u_gauss clipped
    u_new = np.clip(u + rng.normal(0, sigma), 0,1)
    v=a.copy(); v[i]=u_new*t; v[j]=t-u_new*t
    # also block/smooth perturb sometimes
    m=fast_cmax(v)
    if m<best_m: update, sigma*=1.05
    else: if it%10==0 sigma*=0.98
```
Normal pair move preserves sum and nonneg unless t. This is local. Could run 300s. But fast_cmax one per iter; maybe 20k iters. Good.

else best=a.copy()

Now, let's inspect L-BFGS gradient correctness in softmax. `g` = gradient of smooth M wrt a (sum=1). For parameter x -> a_i=e_i/S. ∂M/∂x_i = S(∂M/∂a_i) - a_i S ∂M/∂a? Actually a = e/S, with S=∑e. ∂a_j/∂x_i = a_i(δ_{ij} - a_j). So grad_x = ∑_j g_j a_i(δ_{ij}-a_j) = a_i(g_i - ∑g_j a_j). Yes. Previous used `a*(grad_a - dot)`. Good.

Golden search details:
```
def golden_minimize(a, i, j, t, x0=None, maxfev=25):
    gr = (np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    aL=0.0; aR=t
    x1 = aR - gr*(aR-aL)
    x2 = aL + gr*(aR-aL)
    f1=eval(x1); f2=eval(x2)
    for _ in range(maxfev-2):
       if f1 <= f2:
          aR=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=aR - gr*(aR-aL); f1=eval(x1)
       else:
          aL=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=aL + gr*(aR-aL); f2=eval(x2)
    x=0.5*(aL+aR); f=eval(x)
    # compare with current maybe
    return x,f
```

Nonnegative deconvolution square root via multiplicative updates:
We want a*a = h. Suppose h>0. Consider finding max-likelihood estimator for parameters a_i of a Poisson model for counts? The aggregate count Y_k = ∑_{i,j: i+j=k} X_{ij}? But X_{ij} not independent? There is a known multiplicative update for finding nonnegative square root of a convolution: `a_i <- a_i * ( (h * g)_i / (a * g)_i )`? Let's derive using convex optimization: minimize ∑_k h_k log(h_k/c_k) + ∑? The Bregman divergence for nonnegative bilinear. The IRLS update for nonnegative `c = a ⊗ a` (bilinear) might be:
```
a_i^{new} = a_i * sum_{k} h_k * a_{k-i} / c_k
```
Since derivative of D(h||c) wrt log a_i = ∑ h_k (∂ log c_k / ∂ log a_i) = ∑ h_k (∂c_k/∂a_i)/c_k = ∑ h_k * 2 a_{k-i}/c_k. Setting gradient of Kullback-Leibler? The update `a_i *= exp(-step * grad)` for KL? For KL D(h||c), minimizing over a, gradient wrt log a_i = ∑ h_k (2a_{k-i})/c_k. The natural gradient step `a_i *= a_i / (1/2 ∑ h_k a_{k-i}/c_k)`? Not.

If c=h fixed point, S_i = ∑_k h_k a_{k-i}/c_k =1. So update `a_i *= (0.5 + 0.5*S_i)` drives to fixed point. This is plausible.

Let's implement `conv_root_em(a, h, iters, blend)`:
```
def conv_root_em(a, h, steps=100, blend=0.5):
   n=len(a); N...
   h_sum = h.sum()
   # scale h to sum1
   h = h/h_sum
   eps=1e-16
   for s in range(steps):
      c=fast_convolve(a)
      c_safe=np.maximum(c, eps)
      r=h/c_safe
      fr=rfft(r,N); fa=rfft(a,N)
      S=irfft(conj(fa)*fr,N)[:n]
      S=np.maximum(S,0)
      a = a * (1 - blend + blend* (0.5 + 0.5*S))  # or a * (0.5+0.5*S)
      a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum()
   return a
```
Need choose h target. We can use h = c with peaks clipped to T and normalized. Let M=c.max(), T=max(M*0.9, fast average?). Actually to lower max, set h_k = min(c_k, T) then normalize. But if T<M, total may drop; scaling up may increase some below T to >T? Maybe use iterative target equal current M? If h = c (current), fixed point no change (S_i=1 if r=1). If h = target lower, S_i<1 for high-contributing? Let's reason: If c_k high, r_k small, S_i small for indices contributing to high c, so a_i decreases after multiply. Good. If c_k low, r_k large, a_i increases. Normalization.

def target_from_c(c, M, T):
   h = np.minimum(c, T)
   # ensure positive? maybe h = h + 1e-20

Implement `em_steps(a, h, steps, blend)`:
```
n=len(a); N=get_fft_len(n)
for _ in range(steps):
  fa=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
  c_safe=np.maximum(c, 1e-18)
  r=h/c_safe
  fr=rfft(r,N)
  S=irfft(conj(fa)*fr,N)[:n].real
  S=np.maximum(S, 0.0)
  # normalize S to mean1? If h,sum not 1? Maybe S mean not 1. Use factor = 1/mean(S+eps)
  smean=S.mean()
  if smean>0: S = S/smean  # so update multiplicative centered, preserve approximate sum before normalize
  a = a * (1.0 - blend + blend*S)
  a = np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
return a
```

Implement `trim_to_mass` robust:
```
def trim_to_mass(a, mass=0.9999):
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    total=a.sum()
    if total <= 1e-300: return a
    n=len(a)
    target=float(mass)*total
    if target >= total - 1e-15*total:
        # full support: find first and last positive
        idx=np.nonzero(a > 0)[0]
        if len(idx)==0: return a
        return a[idx[0]:idx[-1]+1]
    cum=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(a)))
    i=0; best_l=0; best_r=n-1; best_len=n+1
    for j in range(n):
        while i <= j and cum[j+1] - cum[i] < target:
            i += 1
        if i <= j:
            length = j - i + 1
            if length < best_len:
                best_len=length; best_l=i; best_r=j
    if best_len <= n:
        return a[best_l:best_r+1]
    return a
```
If target near total, window may be full. For mass=1, use nonzero.
For m masses, if target is 99.99%, maybe excludes tails and reduces n. Good.

 return normalize_nonneg(b)
```

def gaussian_smooth(a, sigma_frac=0.02):
   n=len(a); sigma=max(1.0, n*sigma_frac); half=int(4*sigma)
   x=np.arange(-half, half+1); k=np.exp(-0.5*(x/sigma)**2); k/=k.sum()
   out=np.convolve(a,k,'same')
   # for even? mode same length n
   return normalize_nonneg(out)
```

```
def lse_weights_from_c(c, cmax, tau):
   L=len(c)
   if tau <= max(1e-14, cmax*1e-12):
      tol = max(1e-15*cmax, 1e-18)
      mask = c >= cmax - tol
      if not mask.any(): mask[np.argmax(c)] = True
      w=mask.astype(float)/mask.sum()
      return w, cmax
   x=(c-cmax)/tau
   e=np.zeros(L)
   mask=x > -700
   e[mask]=np.exp(x[mask])
   s=e.sum()
   if s == 0: # fallback top
      mask=x>=0? Actually x max 0 at max; choose c==cmax.
   else:
      w=e/s
      val = cmax + tau*np.log(s)
      return w,val
```

def smooth_anneal(a, deadline):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; best_m=fast_cmax(a)
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        M=fast_cmax(a)
        tau=max(1e-9, 0.1*M)
        cur,best_cur,m_best = lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, maxtime=min(60, deadline-now))
        if m_best < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m=m_best; best=best_cur.copy(); no_improve=0
        else: no_improve +=1
        # choose next a: if current LSE improved but exact not, maybe use cur? Use cur if m_cur <= M*1.05 else best
        cur_m=fast_cmax(cur)
        if cur_m <= M * 1.02 + 1e-13:
            a=cur
        else:
            a=best_cur if m_best < M else a
        if no_improve > 3:
            break
    return best
```

`bundle_polish` implementation in detail:
```
def bundle_polish(a, deadline):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_m=fast_cmax(a)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_fft_len(n)
    p_list=[1,3,7,15,30,75,150,300,600,1200,2400]
    p_list=[p for p in p_list if 1<=p<L]
    if not p_list: p_list=[1]
    base_alphas=[0.03,0.01,0.003,0.001,0.0003]
    alpha_scale=1.0
    no_improve=0
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(98765)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if no_improve > 2000: break
        c_a=fast_convolve(a)
        M=float(c_a.max())
        if M < best_m - 1e-16:
            best_m=M; best=a.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
        order=np.argsort(c_a)[::-1]
        max_p=min(p_list[-1], L-1)
        best_trial_m=M
        best_trial=None
        # directions
        for p in p_list:
            w=np.zeros(L)
            w[order[:p]]=1.0/p
            gw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
            g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*gw,N)[:n].real
            d=-(g - g.mean())
            dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
            if dn < 1e-14: continue
            # Standardize d? use unnormalized. Maybe scale by? We can scale to avoid too large: d = d / max(1.0, dn/np.sqrt(n))? Hmm.
```

Now, `pair_coordinate` with golden:
```
def pair_golden(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, N= None, maxfev=16):
    L=0.0; R=t
    gr=(np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    x1 = R - gr*(R-L)
    x2 = L + gr*(R-L)
    f1=pair_eval(... x1)
    f2=pair_eval(... x2)
    evals=2
    while evals < maxfev and (R-L) > 1e-8*max(1,t):
       if f1 <= f2:
          R=x2; f2=f1; x2=x1; f2=f1? Wait need correct.
```
Standard golden:
```

Detailed functions:

```
def _ensure_array(a):
    if isinstance(a, list): a=np.array(a,float)
    else: a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.ndim !=1: a=a.ravel()
    return a
```
Top-level.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s
```

`trim_to_mass`: as above. Need finite a.

`fast_convolve`:
```
def fast_convolve(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=next_fft_len(n)
    if n <= 200:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
       return np.maximum(c[:L],0.0)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
But if N provided smaller than L? ensure N>=L; caller. For direct small, N not used.

`fast_cmax`:
```
def fast_cmax(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return float('inf')
    if n <= 200:
       c=np.convolve(a,a); return float(np.maximum(c,0).max())
    if N is None: N=next_fft_len(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
    return float(np.maximum(c,0.0).max())
```
If a has negative small, max after clip; but pair_eval nonneg. For score, should consider sum? fast_score uses sum.

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S < 0.01: return inf
    m=fast_cmax(a, N)
    return float(2*n*m/(S*S))
```
If a sum=1 in most use.

`next_fft_len` with cache.

`softmax`:
```
def softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    xm=x-x.max()
    e=np.exp(xm); s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
    return e/s
```

`lse_weights_from_c` returns tuple.
```
def lse_weights_from_c(c, cmax, tau):
    L=len(c)
    if tau <= max(1e-15, 1e-12*max(cmax,1e-300)):
       tol=max(1e-12*max(cmax,1e-300),1e-18)
       mask = c >= cmax - tol
       if not np.any(mask): mask[np.argmax(c)]=True
       w=mask.astype(np.float64)/float(mask.sum())
       return w, float(cmax)
    x=(c-cmax)/tau
    e=np.zeros_like(c)
    mask = x > -700.0
    e[mask] = np.exp(x[mask])
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       # top max exact
       mask = c >= cmax - 1e-12*max(cmax,1e-300)
       if not np.any(mask): mask[np.argmax(c)]=True
       w=mask.astype(np.float64)/float(mask.sum())
       return w, float(cmax)
    w=e/s
    val = float(cmax + tau*np.log(s))
    return w,val
```
If tau small and many equal max within tol, LSE exact approx. Good.

`lse_obj_grad`:
```
def lse_obj_grad(a, tau, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=next_fft_len(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]; c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    w,val=lse_weights_from_c(c,cmax,tau)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw,N)[:n].real
    return val,g,c,cmax
```

`_lbfgs_obj`:
```
def _lbfgs_obj(x):
    a=softmax(x)
    val,_,_,_ = lse_obj_grad(a, _LSE_TAU)
    return val
```
`_lbfgs_grad`: similar. Need if `_LSE_TAU` is too small? okay.

`lbfgs_optimize`:
```
def lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, maxtime):
    global _LSE_TAU
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if len(a)==0: return a,a,float('inf')
    _LSE_TAU=float(tau)
    current=a.copy()
    best=a.copy()
    best_m=fast_cmax(current)
    x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(1.0, maxtime)
    n=len(a)
    # scale maxfun by n? For n large, 250; small 800
    maxfun = 1500 if n < 512 else (700 if n < 4096 else 400)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       try:
          res = minimize(_lbfgs_obj, x, jac=_lbfgs_grad, method='L-BFGS-B',
                         options={'maxiter': max(50, maxfun//2), 'maxfun': maxfun,
                                  'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':30})
       except Exception:
          break
       current=softmax(res.x)
       m=fast_cmax(current)
       if m < best_m - 1e-15:
           best_m=m; best=current.copy()
       # if not improved and status says converged, break
       if res.status in (0, 1, 2, 4):
           # if current not best? if m <= best_m*1.001 use current for next tau? But function return best? We'll return current, best, best_m
           # break only if no time or converged? To avoid stuck, maybe continue if time large? But same tau won't improve.
           break
       # if didn't converge but small improvement, continue with updated x
    return current, best, best_m
```
Potential issue: res.status 1 is? According scipy: 0 success, 1? Could be maxfunction. If it breaks, okay (one call per tau). For quick_smooth with multiple tau, enough. For smooth_anneal dynamic, next tau different.

`smooth_anneal(a, deadline)`:
```
def smooth_anneal(a, deadline):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; best_m=fast_cmax(a); no=0
   while time<deadline:
      M=fast_cmax(a)
      if M < best_m: update
      tau=max(1e-10, 0.05*M)
      alloc = min(80.0, (deadline - time.monotonic())/2)
      if alloc < 3: break
      cur, cur_best, m_cur = lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, alloc)
      if m_cur < best_m - 1e-15:
          best_m=m_cur; best=cur_best.copy(); no=0
      else: no += 1
      cur_m=fast_cmax(cur)
      if cur_m <= M*1.05 + 1e-13:
          a=cur
      else:
          a=cur_best if m_cur < M else a
      if no >= 3: break
   return best
```
If M unchanged and cur_m >M*1.05, keep a/best. Good.

`make_target_h`:
```
def make_target_h(c, T):
    c=np.asarray(c,float); L=len(c)
    if T <=0: T=c.mean()
    h=np.minimum(c, T)
    mean=c.mean()
    floor=max(1e-14, 1e-8*mean)
    h=h+floor
    s=h.sum()
    if s <= 0: h=np.ones(L)/L
    else: h=h/s
    return h
```
If T very small, floor dominates. Fine.

`em_steps`:
```
def em_steps(a, h, steps=8, blend=0.2):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_fft_len(n)
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    for _ in range(steps):
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
        c=np.maximum(c,1e-20)
        r=h/c
        fr=np.fft.rfft(r,N)
        S=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fr,N)[:n].real
        S=np.maximum(S,0.0)
        # prevent extremes
        mean=S.mean()
        if mean > 0:
            S = S / mean
        S = np.clip(S, 1e-3, 1000.0)
        a = a * (1.0 - blend + blend * S)
        a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
        s=a.sum()
        if s <= 1e-300: break
        a = a/s
    return a
```
Maybe mean S not 1 if h sum1? Let's check fixed h=c: r=1, S_i=sum a=1, mean1. Good. If h normalized, S mean? ∑S_i = ∑_k h_k ∑_i a_{k-i} = ∑_k h_k (n? no, for each k, sum over i valid a_{k-i} may not equal total for edges). For full all h uniform, S_i is moving sum of a over [i,i+n-1]; average over i = (1/n)∑ S = average over pairs? Since ∑_i S_i = ∑_k h_{k-i}? Might equal sum h if h constant across overlap? Edges. Not exactly 1. Normalizing S mean stabilizes. Could distort fixed point if h=c edges? But fine.

Line search:
```
for alpha in alphas:
   v = normalize_nonneg(a + alpha*d)
   m = fast_cmax(v, N)

Implement golden:
```
def pair_golden(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, N=None, maxfev=16):
    lo=0.0; hi=float(t)
    if hi <= 0: return old_i, fast_cmax(a,N)
    gr=(np.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    x1=hi - gr*(hi-lo); x2=lo + gr*(hi-lo)
    f1=pair_eval(... x1); f2=pair_eval(... x2)
    best_x=x1; best_f=f1
    if f2<best_f: best_x=x2; best_f=f2
    evals=2
    while evals < maxfev and (hi-lo) > 1e-10*max(1.0,hi):
       if f1 <= f2:
          hi=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=hi - gr*(hi-lo); f1=pair_eval(...x1)
       else:
          lo=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=lo + gr*(hi-lo); f2=pair_eval(...x2)
       evals +=1
       if f1 < best_f: best_x=x1; best_f=f1
       if f2 < best_f: best_x=x2; best_f=f2
    # final midpoint
    xm=0.5*(lo+hi); fm=pair_eval(...xm); evals++
    if fm<best_f: best_x=xm; best_f=fm
    return best_x,best_f
```
Good.

def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    time_limit = max(120, int(budget_s) - 90) # if budget 2400, 2310
    deadline = t0 + time_limit

    # allocate smooth 30%, em 15%, bundle 35%, pair 20% of time_left, but at least.

    add pool
    em_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(60, 0.12*time_left))
    a_opt = em_target_polish(a_opt, em_deadline)

    a_opt = bundle_polish(a_opt, bundle_deadline)

    pair_deadline = deadline - 5
    a_opt = pair_coordinate(a_opt, pair_deadline)

    # postprocess trim best pool candidates: take internal best from pool, try trims add pool
    # final evaluate pool

`gaussian_smooth`: If kernel length huge? half int. `np.convolve(a,k,'same')` length n? For even/odd kernel, mode same returns length max(M,N)? For 1D, mode='same' returns output length max(len(a), len(k))? Actually numpy convolve same returns length max(M,N). If kernel longer than a, returns length kernel >n. To be safe, ensure kernel length < a? sigma_frac 0.02 => half=96 for n=4806, kernel193 <n. If n small < kernel, could mode same length kernel. We can if len(out)!=n, center crop/pad. Simpler: Use FFT circular smooth? Or truncate sigma. In gaussian_smooth, `half = int(min(4*sigma, max(1, n//4)))`; kernel length <= n/2+1, so same returns n. Good. For n small, n//4 maybe 0; max1. Use `sigma = max(1.0, min(n*sigma_frac, n/8?))`. Let's implement robust:
```
def gaussian_smooth(a, sigma_frac=0.02):
    n=len(a)
    if n<2: return a
    sigma = max(1.0, n*sigma_frac)
    half = int(min(4.0*sigma, 0.25*n))
    if half < 1: half=1
    x=np.arange(-half, half+1)
    k=np.exp(-0.5*(x/sigma)**2)
    k/=k.sum()
    out=np.convolve(a,k,'same')
    if len(out) != n:
       # center
       out = out[(len(out)-n)//2 : (len(out)-n)//2 + n]
    return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
Good.

`generate_initial`:
```
def generate_initial(n, rng):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    phase1=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); phase2=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    a=1.0 + 0.4*np.cos(2*np.pi*n? no freq=1) + 0.3*np.cos(4*np.pi*i/n+phase2)
    a = a + 0.2*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
    a = gaussian_smooth(a, 0.01)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)
```
Maybe if no parent and n=4096, score ~2; but okay.

`get_parent_sequence`:
```
def get_parent_sequence():
    val = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if val is not None:
       try: a=np.asarray(val,float)
       except: a=None
       if a is not None and a.size>0:
           a=np.nan_to_num(...)
           a=np.maximum(a,0)
           s=a.sum()
           if s>1e-12:
               return normalize_nonneg(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    return generate_initial(4096, rng)
```

Now, `make_variants(base, rng)`:
```

        if v.size>0: raw.append(v)
    # sym
    raw.append(0.5*(base + base[::-1]))
    raw.append(gaussian_smooth(base, 0.02))
    raw.append(gaussian_smooth(base, 0.005))
    for q in (2,3):
        raw.append(downsample_blocks(base,q))
    # if base length >3000, maybe random smoothing? No.
    # random inits with length around base/2? maybe if time.
    if rng is not None:
        n2 = max(512, min(4096, len(base)//2))
        raw.append(gaussian_smooth(generate_initial(n2, rng), 0.01))
    scored=[]
    for v in raw:
        v=normalize_nonneg(v)
        if v.size<2: continue
        # trim zeros
        v=trim_to_mass(v,1.0)
        s=fast_score(v)
        if np.isfinite(s):
           # best per len
           dup=False
           for bs,bv in scored:
              if bv.size == v.size and s > bs: dup=True; break
           if not dup: scored.append((s,v))
    scored=sort_pairs_by_score(scored)
    return scored
```

def quick_smooth(a, maxtime):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size==0 or maxtime < 1.0: return a
    best=a.copy(); best_m=fast_cmax(a)
    start=time.monotonic()
    M=best_m
    taus=[M, 0.3*M, 0.08*M, 0.02*M]
    # filter tau > 1e-12
    taus=[t for t in taus if t > 1e-12] # list comprehension okay
    for idx,tau in enumerate(taus):
        rem = start + maxtime - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 3: break
        alloc = min(rem, max(5.0, maxtime/len(taus)))
        cur,cur_best,m_cur=lbfgs_optimize(a, max(1e-12,tau), max(1.0, alloc))
        if m_cur < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m=m_cur; best=cur_best.copy()
        # For next tau, use cur if not too bad
        cur_m=fast_cmax(cur)
        if cur_m <= M*1.1 + 1e-12:
            a=cur
        else:
            a=cur_best if m_cur < M else a
        M=best_m
    return best

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Strategy:
1. Start from height_sequence_1 when available, plus simple variants
   (trimmed, symmetrized, smoothed, block-downsampled, random).
2. Smooth optimization with L-BFGS on a log-sum-exp smoothed maximum.
3. An experimental nonnegative convolution square-root EM style polish
   driven by a clipped target convolution.
4. Nonsmooth bundle/subgradient polish using top-k and temperature weights.
5. Exact two-coordinate golden-section polish.
6. Final crop/trim post-processing and exact evaluation of the best
   internal candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_FFT_CACHE = {}
_LSE_TAU = 1e-6


def next_fft_len(n):
    if n in _FFT_CACHE:
        return _FFT_CACHE[n]
    L = 2 * int(n) - 1
    p = 1
    while p < L:
        p <<= 1
    _FFT_CACHE[int(n)] = p
    return p


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def trim_to_mass(a, mass=0.9999):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    total = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or total <= 1e-300:
        return a

    if mass >= 1.0 - 1e-12:
        idx = np.nonzero(a > 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            return a
        return a[idx[0]:idx[-1] + 1]

    target = float(mass) * total
    if target <= 0.0:
        return a

    cum = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cum[0] = 0.0
    cum[1:] = np.cumsum(a)

    i = 0
    best_l = 0
    best_r = n - 1
    best_len = n + 1

    for j in range(n):
        while i <= j and (cum[j + 1] - cum[i]) < target:
            i += 1
        if i <= j:
            cur_len = j - i + 1
            if cur_len < best_len:
                best_len = cur_len
                best_l = i
                best_r = j

    if best_len <= n:
        return a[best_l:best_r + 1]
    return a


def downsample_blocks(a, q):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    m = n // int(q)
    if m < 8:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    b = a[:m * int(q)].reshape(m, int(q)).sum(axis=1)
    return normalize_nonneg(b)


def gaussian_smooth(a, sigma_frac=0.02):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a
    sigma = max(1.0, float(sigma_frac) * float(n))
    half = int(min(4.0 * sigma, 0.25 * float(n)))
    if half < 1:
        half = 1
    x = np.arange(-half, half + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    k = np.exp(-0.5 * (x / sigma) ** 2)
    ksum = float(k.sum())
    if ksum <= 0.0:
        k = np.ones_like(k) / float(k.size)
    else:
        k /= ksum
    out = np.convolve(a, k, mode="same")
    if out.size != n:
        off = max(0, (out.size - n) // 2)
        out = out[off:off + n]
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def generate_initial(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    a = (
        1.0
        + 0.45 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * i / float(max(1, n)) + p1)
        + 0.30 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * i / float(max(1, n)) + p2)
        + 0.18 * np.sin(8.0 * np.pi * i / float(max(1, n)) + p1)
    )
    a = a + 0.15 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
    a = gaussian_smooth(a, 0.012)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def get_parent_sequence():
    val = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if val is not None:
        try:
            a = np.asarray(val, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            a = None
        if a is not None and a.size > 0:
            a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            if float(a.sum()) > 1e-12:
                return normalize_nonneg(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    return generate_initial(4096, rng)


def fast_convolve(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if n <= 220:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        return np.maximum(c[:L], 0.0)

    if N is None:
        N = next_fft_len(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def fast_cmax(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")

    if n <= 220:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        return float(np.maximum(c, 0.0).max())

    if N is None:
        N = next_fft_len(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    return float(np.maximum(c, 0.0).max())


def fast_score(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    M = fast_cmax(a, N)
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * M / (S * S))


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - float(x.max())
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def lse_weights_from_c(c, cmax, tau):
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0

    tiny = 1e-18
    scale = max(float(cmax), 1e-300)

    if tau <= max(1e-14, 1e-12 * scale):
        tol = max(1e-11 * scale, 1e-18)
        mask = c >= (cmax - tol)
        if not np.any(mask):
            mask = c >= cmax - tol
        if not np.any(mask):
            mask = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)
            mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
        w = mask.astype(np.float64)
        w /= float(w.sum())
        return w, float(cmax)

    x = (c - cmax) / tau
    e = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = x > -700.0
    e[mask] = np.exp(x[mask])
    s = float(e.sum())

    if s <= 1e-300:
        tol = max(1e-11 * scale, 1e-18)
        mask = c >= (cmax - tol)
        if not np.any(mask):
            mask = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)
            mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
        w = mask.astype(np.float64)
        w /= float(w.sum())
        return w, float(cmax)

    w = e / s
    val = float(cmax + tau * np.log(s))
    return w, val


def lse_obj_grad(a, tau, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = next_fft_len(n)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    w, val = lse_weights_from_c(c, cmax, tau)

    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fw, N)[:n].real
    return val, g, c, cmax


def _lbfgs_obj(x):
    a = softmax(x)
    val, _, _, _ = lse_obj_grad(a, _LSE_TAU)
    return float(val)


def _lbfgs_grad(x):
    a = softmax(x)
    _, g, _, _ = lse_obj_grad(a, _LSE_TAU)
    return a * (g - float(np.dot(a, g)))


def lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, maxtime):
    global _LSE_TAU

    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or maxtime <= 0.5:
        return a, a, fast_cmax(a)

    _LSE_TAU = max(1e-15, float(tau))

    current = a.copy()
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = fast_cmax(current)

    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(maxtime)

    if n > 20000:
        maxfun = 140
    elif n > 2000:
        maxfun = 320
    elif n > 500:
        maxfun = 700
    else:
        maxfun = 2000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_obj,
                x,
                jac=_lbfgs_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(40, maxfun // 2),
                    "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        current = softmax(res.x)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(current)):
            break

        m = fast_cmax(current)
        if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best = current.copy()

        # If the solver reports terminal success or similar, stop retrying same tau.
        if res.status in (0, 1, 2, 4):
            break

    return current, best, best_m


def quick_smooth(a, maxtime):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or maxtime < 2.0:
        return a

    start = time.monotonic()
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = fast_cmax(a)
    M = best_m

    taus = [M, 0.35 * M, 0.10 * M, 0.03 * M]
    taus = [float(t) for t in taus if np.isfinite(t) and t > 1e-13]
    if not taus:
        return best

    time_each = max(4.0, float(maxtime) / float(len(taus)))

    for tau in taus:
        rem = start + float(maxtime) - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 3.0:
            break
        alloc = min(rem, time_each)
        if alloc < 2.0:
            continue

        cur, cur_best, m_cur = lbfgs_optimize(a, max(1e-13, tau), alloc)

        if np.isfinite(m_cur) and m_cur < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m_cur
            best = cur_best.copy()

        cur_m = fast_cmax(cur)
        if np.isfinite(cur_m) and cur_m <= M * 1.10 + 1e-12:
            a = cur
        elif np.isfinite(m_cur) and m_cur < M:
            a = cur_best
        else:
            a = best

        M = best_m

    return best


def make_target_h(c, T):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return c
    if T <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        T = float(c.mean())
    h = np.minimum(c, float(T))
    mean = float(c.mean())
    floor = max(1e-14, 1e-9 * max(mean, 1e-300))
    h = h + floor
    s = float(h.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        h = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        h /= s
    return h


def em_steps(a, h, steps=8, blend=0.18):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or steps <= 0:
        return a
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_fft_len(n)

    for _ in range(int(steps)):
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 1e-20)

        r = h / c
        fr = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
        S = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fr, N)[:n].real
        S = np.maximum(S, 0.0)

        mean = float(S.mean())
        if mean > 0.0:
            S /= mean
        S = np.clip(S, 1e-3, 1000.0)

        a = a * (1.0 - float(blend) + float(blend) * S)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break
        a /= s

    return a


def em_target_polish(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    best_m = fast_cmax(a)

    target_score = 1.50
    target_m = max(1e-15, target_score / (2.0 * float(n)))
    T = max(1e-15, min(0.98 * best_m, max(target_m * 0.999, 1e-12)))
    no_improve = 0
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < best_m - 1e-16:
            best_m = M
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 0:
            T = min(M, max(target_m * 1.0001, T * 1.18 + 1e-16))

        h = make_target_h(c, T)
        a2 = em_steps(a, h, steps=7, blend=0.18)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a2)):
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8:
                break
            continue

        m2 = fast_cmax(a2)
        if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < M - 1e-15:
            a = a2
            if m2 < best_m - 1e-16:
                best_m = m2
                best = a.copy()
            T = max(1e-15, min(0.97 * M, max(target_m * 0.999, T * 0.88)))
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8:
                break

        if start + 1.0 > time.monotonic() and int((time.monotonic() - start) % 30) == 0:
            print(f"  EM polish M={best_m:.8e} internal_score={2.0*float(n)*best_m:.8f}")

    return best


def bundle_polish(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_fft_len(n)

    best = a.copy()
    best_m = fast_cmax(a, N)

    if L < 3:
        return best

    p_list = [1, 3, 7, 15, 30, 75, 150, 300, 600, 1200, 2400]
    p_list = [int(p) for p in p_list if 1 <= p < L]
    if not p_list:
        p_list = [1]

    base_alphas = np.array([0.5, 0.15, 0.05, 0.015, 0.004], dtype=np.float64)
    alpha_scale = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if no_improve > 8000:
            break

        c_a = fast_convolve(a, N)
        M = float(c_a.max())

        if M < best_m - 1e-16:
            best_m = M
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 25.0:
            print(f"  bundle best M={best_m:.8e} internal_score={2.0*float(n)*best_m:.8f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        order = np.argsort(c_a)[::-1]

        best_trial_m = M
        best_trial = None

        # Top-k uniform active-set directions.
        for p in p_list:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[order[:p]] = 1.0 / float(p)
            gw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
            g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * gw, N)[:n].real
            d = -(g - float(g.mean()))

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                continue

            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
            if rms <= 1e-16:
                continue

            d *= (1.0 / float(n)) / rms
            alphas = base_alphas * alpha_scale

            for alpha in alphas:
                v = normalize_nonneg(a + float(alpha) * d)
                m = fast_cmax(v, N)
                if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_trial_m:
                    best_trial_m = m
                    best_trial = v

        # Temperature/LSE directions.
        for frac in (0.05, 0.20, 0.80):
            tau = max(1e-13, M * float(frac))
            w, _ = lse_weights_from_c(c_a, M, tau)
            gw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
            g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * gw, N)[:n].real
            d = -(g - float(g.mean()))

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                continue

            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
            if rms <= 1e-16:
                continue

            d *= (1.0 / float(n)) / rms
            alphas = base_alphas * alpha_scale

            for alpha in alphas:
                v = normalize_nonneg(a + float(alpha) * d)
                m = fast_cmax(v, N)
                if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_trial_m:
                    best_trial_m = m
                    best_trial = v

        if best_trial is not None:
            a = best_trial
            no_improve = 0
            alpha_scale = min(6.0, alpha_scale * 1.08)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            alpha_scale = max(0.05, alpha_scale * 0.75)

            # Occasional random tangent perturbation to avoid stuck directions.
            if no_improve % 25 == 0:
                d = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
                d -= float(d.mean())
                rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
                if rms > 1e-16:
                    d *= (1.0 / float(n)) / rms
                    for alpha in (0.05, 0.015, 0.004):
                        v = normalize_nonneg(a + alpha * d)
                        m = fast_cmax(v, N)
                        if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_trial_m:
                            best_trial_m = m
                            best_trial = v

                    if best_trial is not None:
                        a = best_trial
                        no_improve = 0
                        alpha_scale = min(6.0, alpha_scale * 1.2)
                    else:
                        alpha_scale = max(0.05, alpha_scale * 0.8)

    return best


def pair_eval(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, x, N=None):
    if x < 0.0:
        x = 0.0
    elif x > t:
        x = t
    a[i] = float(x)
    a[j] = float(t - x)
    try:
        return fast_cmax(a, N)
    finally:
        a[i] = float(old_i)
        a[j] = float(old_j)


def pair_golden(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, N=None, maxfev=16):
    lo = 0.0
    hi = float(t)
    if hi <= 0.0:
        return float(old_i), fast_cmax(a, N)

    gr = (np.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    x1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
    x2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)

    f1 = pair_eval(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, x1, N)
    f2 = pair_eval(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, x2, N)

    best_x = float(x1)
    best_f = float(f1)
    if f2 < best_f:
        best_x = float(x2)
        best_f = float(f2)

    evals = 2
    while evals < int(maxfev) and (hi - lo) > 1e-11 * max(1.0, hi):
        if f1 <= f2:
            hi = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
            f1 = pair_eval(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, x1, N)
        else:
            lo = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            f2 = pair_eval(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, x2, N)

        evals += 1
        if f1 < best_f:
            best_f = float(f1)
            best_x = float(x1)
        if f2 < best_f:
            best_f = float(f2)
            best_x = float(x2)

    xm = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    fm = pair_eval(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, xm, N)
    if fm < best_f:
        best_f = float(fm)
        best_x = float(xm)

    return best_x, best_f


def pair_coordinate(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a

    N = next_fft_len(n)
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = fast_cmax(a, N)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(543219876)
    no_improve = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if no_improve > 8000:
            break

        i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        j = int(rng.integers(0, n - 1))
        if j >= i:
            j -= 1

        old_i = float(a[i])
        old_j = float(a[j])
        t = old_i + old_j
        if t <= 1e-13:
            continue

        x_opt, f_opt = pair_golden(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, N, maxfev=15)

        if np.isfinite(f_opt) and f_opt < best_m - 1e-15:
            a[i] = float(x_opt)
            a[j] = float(t - x_opt)
            best_m = float(f_opt)
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 25.0:
            print(f"  pairs best M={best_m:.8e} internal_score={2.0*float(n)*best_m:.8f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        if start + 1.0 > time.monotonic() and int((time.monotonic() - start) % 200) == 0:
            # Very occasionally sample a pair with larger total mass by biased coordinate.
            idx = int(np.argmax(a))
            i = idx
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n - 1))
            if j >= i:
                j -= 1
            old_i = float(a[i])
            old_j = float(a[j])
            t = old_i + old_j
            if t > 1e-13:
                x_opt, f_opt = pair_golden(a, i, j, old_i, old_j, t, N, maxfev=12)
                if np.isfinite(f_opt) and f_opt < best_m - 1e-15:
                    a[i] = float(x_opt)
                    a[j] = float(t - x_opt)
                    best_m = float(f_opt)
                    best = a.copy()
                    no_improve = 0

    return best


def sort_pairs_by_score(pairs):
    lst = list(pairs)
    out = []
    while lst:
        mi = 0
        mv = lst[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(lst)):
            if lst[i][0] < mv:
                mv = lst[i][0]
                mi = i
        out.append(lst.pop(mi))
    return out


def add_to_pool(pool, a, cap=60):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    s = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return

    if len(pool) >= cap:
        min_idx = 0
        min_s = float("inf")
        for i, (bs, _) in enumerate(pool):
            if bs < min_s:
                min_s = bs
                min_idx = i
        if s >= min_s - 1e-12:
            return
        pool[min_idx] = (s, a.copy())
    else:
        pool.append((s, a.copy()))


def make_variants(base, rng):
    base = normalize_nonneg(base)
    raw = []

    for mass in (1.0, 0.99999, 0.9999, 0.999, 0.995, 0.99):
        v = trim_to_mass(base, mass)
        if v.size > 0:
            raw.append(v)

    raw.append(0.5 * (base + base[::-1]))
    raw.append(gaussian_smooth(base, 0.020))
    raw.append(gaussian_smooth(base, 0.006))

    for q in (2, 3):
        raw.append(downsample_blocks(base, q))

    if rng is not None:
        n2 = max(512, min(4096, base.size // 2))
        raw.append(generate_initial(n2, rng))
        n3 = max(1024, min(8192, base.size))
        raw.append(generate_initial(n3, rng))

    scored = []
    for v in raw:
        v = normalize_nonneg(v)
        if v.size < 2:
            continue
        v = trim_to_mass(v, 1.0)
        if v.size == 0:
            continue
        s = fast_score(v)
        if not np.isfinite(s):
            continue

        duplicate = False
        for bs, bv in scored:
            if bv.size == v.size and s > bs:
                duplicate = True
                break
        if not duplicate:
            scored.append((s, v))

    scored = sort_pairs_by_score(scored)
    if len(scored) > 8:
        scored = scored[:8]
    return scored


def evaluate_pool(pool, max_eval=15):
    ordered = sort_pairs_by_score(pool)
    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for s_internal, a in ordered[: max(1, int(max_eval))]:
        seq = [float(x) for x in a]
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq

    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 60.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(float(budget), 2400.0)
    deadline = t0 + max(120.0, budget - 90.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240517)

    pool = []

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    base = trim_to_mass(parent, 1.0)
    base = normalize_nonneg(base)
    add_to_pool(pool, base, cap=80)

    print(f"Initial internal score={fast_score(base):.8f}, n={base.size}")
    try:
        init_exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in base])
        if np.isfinite(init_exact):
            print(f"Initial exact score={init_exact:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if base.size == 0:
        base = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
        add_to_pool(pool, base, cap=80)

    # --- Phase 1: quick exploration over several variants. ---
    phase1_deadline = t0 + min(360.0, 0.22 * (deadline - t0))
    variants = make_variants(base, rng)

    for idx, (vscore, v) in enumerate(variants[:3]):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase1_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        alloc = min(140.0, max(10.0, (phase1_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(1 + idx)))
        opt = quick_smooth(v, alloc)
        add_to_pool(pool, opt, cap=80)
        print(f"Phase1 variant {idx} internal={fast_score(opt):.8f} n={opt.size}")

    best_seed = None
    best_seed_score = float("inf")
    for s, a in pool:
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_seed_score:
            best_seed_score = s
            best_seed = a.copy()

    if best_seed is None or best_seed.size == 0:
        best_seed = base

    print(f"Best seed internal={best_seed_score:.8f}, n={best_seed.size}")

    cur = best_seed.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180.0:
        smooth_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(120.0, min(0.34 * rem, rem - 120.0))
        cur = smooth_anneal_alias(cur, smooth_deadline)
        add_to_pool(pool, cur, cap=80)
        print(f"After smooth internal={fast_score(cur):.8f}, n={cur.size}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0:
        em_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(60.0, 0.16 * rem)
        cur = em_target_polish(cur, em_deadline)
        add_to_pool(pool, cur, cap=80)
        print(f"After EM internal={fast_score(cur):.8f}, n={cur.size}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0:
        bundle_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(120.0, 0.44 * rem)
        cur = bundle_polish(cur, bundle_deadline)
        add_to_pool(pool, cur, cap=80)
        print(f"After bundle internal={fast_score(cur):.8f}, n={cur.size}")

    pair_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, deadline - 8.0)
    cur = pair_coordinate(cur, pair_deadline)
    add_to_pool(pool, cur, cap=80)
    print(f"After pairs internal={fast_score(cur):.8f}, n={cur.size}")

    # Final crops/trim post-processing.
    top_pool = sort_pairs_by_score(pool)[:5]
    for _, a in top_pool:
        for mass in (0.999999, 0.9999, 0.999, 0.995, 0.99, 0.98, 0.95):
            v = trim_to_mass(a, mass)
            v = normalize_nonneg(v)
            if v.size > 0:
                add_to_pool(pool, v, cap=90)
        add_to_pool(pool, 0.5 * (a + a[::-1]), cap=90)

    final_seq, final_score = evaluate_pool(pool, max_eval=18)

    if final_seq is None:
        # Fallback: use best internal sequence and test it directly.
        try:
            final_seq = [float(x) for x in cur]
            final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq))
        except Exception:
            final_seq = [float(x) for x in base]
            final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq))

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_seq = [float(x) for x in base]

    print(f"Final chosen exact score={final_score:.8f}, length={len(final_seq)}")
    return final_seq


# The name is used above but defined at top level to keep helpers top-level only.
def smooth_anneal_alias(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size == 0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    best_m = fast_cmax(a)
    no_improve = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        M = fast_cmax(a)
        if M < best_m - 1e-16:
            best_m = M
            best = a.copy()

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 25.0:
            print(f"  smooth best M={best_m:.8e} internal_score={2.0*float(a.size)*best_m:.8f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        tau = max(1e-12, 0.05 * M)
        alloc = min(70.0, max(5.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / 2.0))
        if alloc < 3.0:
            break

        cur, cur_best, m_best = lbfgs_optimize(a, tau, alloc)

        if np.isfinite(m_best) and m_best < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m_best
            best = cur_best.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        cur_m = fast_cmax(cur)
        if np.isfinite(cur_m) and cur_m <= M * 1.06 + 1e-13:
            a = cur
        elif np.isfinite(m_best) and m_best < M:
            a = cur_best
        else:
            a = best

        if no_improve >= 3:
            break

        if start + 1.0 > time.monotonic() and int((time.monotonic() - start) % 30) == 0:
            pass

    return best
```
